This paper proposes a deductive learning approach for action planning using domain-specific logic constraints, requiring only one example with a full initial and partial goal state to learn effective action models.

2184
This paper introduces a quantum machine learning algorithm for semi-supervised kernel support vector machines, leveraging quantum Hamiltonian simulation to maintain quantum speedup while handling unlabeled data.

767
This paper introduces a novel ranking loss function for RNNs in session-based recommendations, achieving up to 35% improvement in MRR and Recall@20 over previous RNN methods and 51% over classical approaches without significantly increasing training time.

A hierarchical model learns to efficiently place computational graphs on heterogeneous hardware, outperforming human experts and prior methods, achieving up to 60.6% runtime reduction in tasks like Neural Machine Translation.

This paper introduces a new PAC-Bayesian generalization bound for Bayesian neural networks using the negative log-likelihood loss and the Herbst Argument to analyze their risk.

2114
This paper explores using importance sampling for causal reasoning, especially counterfactual inference, within probabilistic programming. It introduces MultiVerse, a prototype engine for approximate causal reasoning, and compares it with Pyro.
